library\0a  -fno-each-lib-rpath            Prevent adding rpath for each used dynamic library\0a  -fallow-shlib-undefined        Allows undefined symbols in shared libraries\0a  -fno-allow-shlib-undefined     Disallows undefined symbols in shared libraries\0a  --build-id[=style]             At a minor link-time expense, coordinates stripped binaries\0a      fast, uuid, sha1, md5      with debug symbols via a '.note.gnu.build-id' section\0a      0x[hexstring]              Maximum 32 bytes\0a      none                       (default) Disable build-id\0a  --eh-frame-hdr                 Enable C++ exception handling by passing --eh-frame-hdr to linker\0a  --emit-relocs                  Enable output of relocation sections for post build tools\0a  -z [arg]                       Set linker extension flags\0a    nodelete                     Indicate that the object cannot be deleted from a process\0a    notext                       Permit read-only relocations in read-only segments\0a    defs                         Force a fatal error if any undefined symbols remain\0a    undefs                       Reverse of -z defs\0a    origin                       Indicate that the object must have its origin processed\0a    nocopyreloc                  Disable the creation of copy relocations\0a    now                          (default) Force all relocations to be processed on load\0a    lazy                         Don't force all relocations to be processed on load\0a    relro                        (default) Force all relocations to be read-only after processing\0a    norelro                      Don't force all relocations to be read-only after processing\0a    common-page-size=[bytes]     Set the common page size for ELF binaries\0a    max-page-size=[bytes]        Set the max page size for ELF binaries\0a  -dynamic                       Force output to be dynamically linked\0a  -static                        Force output to be statically linked\0a  -Bsymbolic                     Bind global references locally\0a  --compress-debug-sections=[e]  Debug section compression settings\0a      none                       No compression\0a      zlib                       Compression with deflate/inflate\0a      zstd                       Compression with zstandard\0a  --gc-sections                  Force removal of functions and data that are unreachable by the entry point or exported symbols\0a  --no-gc-sections               Don't force removal of unreachable functions and data\0a  --sort-section=[value]         Sort wildcard section patterns by 'name' or 'alignment'\0a  --subsystem [subsystem]        (Windows) /SUBSYSTEM:<subsystem> to the linker\0a  --stack [size]                 Override default stack size\0a  --image-base [addr]            Set base address for executable image\0a  -install_name=[value]          (Darwin) add dylib's install name\0a  --entitlements [path]          (Darwin) add path to entitlements file for embedding in code signature\0a  -pagezero_size [value]         (Darwin) size of the __PAGEZERO segment in hexadecimal notation\0a  -headerpad [value]             (Darwin) set minimum space for future expansion of the load commands in hexadecimal notation\0a  -headerpad_max_install_names   (Darwin) set enough space as if all paths were MAXPATHLEN\0a  -dead_strip                    (Darwin) remove functions and data that are unreachable by the entry point or exported symbols\0a  -dead_strip_dylibs             (Darwin) remove dylibs that are unreachable by the entry point or exported symbols\0a  -ObjC                          (Darwin) force load all members of static archives that implement an Objective-C class or category\0a  --import-memory                (WebAssembly) import memory from the environment\0a  --export-memory                (WebAssembly) export memory to the host (Default unless --import-memory used)\0a  --import-symbols               (WebAssembly) import missing symbols from the host environment\0a  --import-table                 (WebAssembly) import function table from the host environment\0a  --export-table                 (WebAssembly) export function table to the host environment\0a  --initial-memory=[bytes]       (WebAssembly) initial size of the linear memory\0a  --max-memory=[bytes]           (WebAssembly) maximum size of the linear memory\0a  --shared-memory                (WebAssembly) use shared linear memory\0a  --global-base=[addr]           (WebAssembly) where to start to place global data\0a\0aPer-Module Link Options:\0a  -l[lib], --library [lib]       Link against system library (only if actually used)\0a  -needed-l[lib],                Link against system library (even if unused)\0a    --needed-library [lib]\0a  -weak-l[lib]                   link against system library marking it and all\0a    -weak_library [lib]          referenced symbols as weak\0a  -L[d], --library-directory [d] Add a directory to the library search path\0a  -search_paths_first            For each library search path, check for dynamic\0a                                 lib then static lib before proceeding to next path.\0a  -search_paths_first_static     For each library search path, check for static\0a                                 lib then dynamic lib before proceeding to next path.\0a  -search_dylibs_first           Search for dynamic libs in all library search\0a                                 paths, then static libs.\0a  -search_static_first           Search for static libs in all library search\0a                                 paths, then dynamic libs.\0a  -search_dylibs_only            Only search for dynamic libs.\0a  -search_static_only            Only search for static libs.\0a  -rpath [path]                  Add directory to -needed_library [lib]          (Darwin) link against system library (even if unused)\0a  -weak_framework [name]         (Darwin) link against framework and mark it and all referenced symbols as [text]           Skip tests that do not match any filter\0a  --test-name-prefix [text]      Add prefix to all tests\0a  --test-cmd [arg]               Specify test execution command one arg at a binary without running it\0a  --test-runner [path]           Specify a custom test runner\0a\0aDebug Options (Zig Compiler Development):\0a  -fopt-bisect-limit=[limit]   Only run [limit] first LLVM
